permission_octal2string ($mode) {\r\n\r\n\tif (($mode & 0xC000) === 0xC000) {\r\n\t\t$type = \'s\';\r\n\t} elseif (($mode & 0xA000) === 0xA000) {\r\n\t\t$type = \'l\';\r\n\t} elseif (($mode & 0x8000) ($filesize) {\r\n\r\n\t$suffices = \'kMGTPE\';\r\n\r\n\t$n = 0;\r\n\twhile ($filesize >= 1000) {\r\n\t\t$filesize /= 1024;\r\n\t\t$n++;\r\n\t}\r\n\r\n\t$filesize = round($filesize, 3 -
